### Task 10: The author's review page, as a private claude.ai Artifact

**Why.** DESIGN section 6 and D19: before a chapter is committed, the author reviews it privately, on a phone, as a
page that takes comments. The author asked to see chapter 1, and any chapter whose acceptance is less certain, as a
private claude.ai Artifact (Artifacts carry comment threads), and no other chapter unless there is a good reason. Open
question: can the boxes' runtime run inside an Artifact page at all? Its files fit (compiler.wasm 4.5 MB,
runtime-classlib-teavm.bin 2.4 MB, compile-classlib-teavm.bin 150 KB, under the 15 MB a binary file), but it needs
module workers, WebAssembly and the page's own scripts loaded beside it, and the Artifact's host decides whether those
work. The scratchpad cannot come at all (jdk.zip is 22 MB). Step 1 answers the question; Steps 2 to 7 build one of two
branches; Step 8 is how the controller uses it for every chapter.

**Files:**
- Throwaway, never committed: `build/.work/review-spike/probe.html`, `build/.work/review-spike/stage.mjs`
- Create: `web/review.mjs` (branch (a) or branch (b), never both)
- Create: `web/test/review.mjs` (its test, for the branch taken)
- Modify, branch (a): `ci/lib.sh:107-113` (`browser_steps`: one Chromium-only step); `README.md` after line 67 (one paragraph)
- Modify, branch (b): `ci/everything.sh` after line 35 (one step); `README.md` after line 67 (one paragraph);
  `DESIGN.md` section 6 (one clause); `DECISIONS.md` (one new decision, the spike's outcome)

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: Task 9's fixture `web/test/vol-accept` and its acceptance results
  (`build/.work/acceptance[-<slug>]/<chNN>/<engine>/results.json`, `<page>.png`, `boxes/<page>-<n>.png`, format in
  Task 9); `web/test/harness.mjs` `REPO`, `buildSite`, `open`, `check`, `done`; the reveal's markup (`.box > details.solution > pre`, the first worked solution); the
  Artifact tool (`publish` with `file_path`, `files`, `icon`, `description`; a republish from the same `file_path`
  keeps the link) and the ArtifactComments tool (deferred: load it with ToolSearch `select:ArtifactComments`), both
  used by the controller only.
- Produces:
  - `node web/review.mjs [--volume <dir>] <chNN>`: writes `build/.work/review/<chNN>/` (another volume:
    `build/.work/review/<slug>-<chNN>/`) holding `index.html`, the files beside it, and `files.json`:
    `{ "file_path": "<absolute path of index.html>", "files": { "<published path>": { "from": "<absolute path>",
    "contentType": "<media type>" } } }`, which is exactly what the Artifact tool's publish takes. Prints
    `review: build/.work/review/<…>/index.html and <N> files beside it (files.json)`; exit 0, 1 on refusal or error, 2 on misuse.
  - The triggers and the controller's procedure (Step 8).

- [ ] **Step 1: Spike: can a box run inside an Artifact?**

Stage one built page of Task 9's fixture (chapter 1: box `#1` prints Hello, `#2` asks a question, `#5` is a method
check), its scripts and the boxes' runtime, every path relative, with a probe that presses the page's own buttons.

`build/.work/review-spike/probe.html`:

```html
<div class="note" id="spike">
<p>Spike: can a box run inside an Artifact? Tap the button, wait for the last line, then copy every line below into a comment on this page.</p>
<button type="button" id="spike-go">Try Run, input and Check</button>
<pre id="spike-out"></pre>
</div>
<script type="module">
const out = document.getElementById("spike-out");
const say = (s) => { out.textContent += s + "\n"; };
const errors = [];
addEventListener("error", (e) => errors.push(String(e.message || e)));
addEventListener("unhandledrejection", (e) => errors.push(String(e.reason)));
const until = (fn, ms) => new Promise((resolve) => {
  const end = performance.now() + ms;
  const tick = () => { const v = fn(); if (v || performance.now() > end) resolve(v); else setTimeout(tick, 100); };
  tick();
});
const box = (id) => document.querySelector('.box[data-box="' + id + '"]');
const press = (b, name) => [...b.querySelectorAll("button")].find((x) => x.textContent === name).click();
const SOLUTION = "boolean isEven(int n) {\n    return n % 2 == 0;\n}\n\nvoid main() {\n    IO.println(isEven(4));\n}\n";
document.getElementById("spike-go").addEventListener("click", async () => {
  out.textContent = "";
  say("origin " + location.origin + "; secure " + isSecureContext + "; isolated " + self.crossOriginIsolated + "; java " + document.documentElement.dataset.java);
  say(navigator.userAgent);
  let worker = "ok";
  try { new Worker(new URL("runner/browser-worker.js?role=probe", location.href), { type: "module" }).terminate(); } catch (e) { worker = String(e); }
  say("module worker: " + worker);
  try {
    const hello = box("ch01-first-steps#1"), t0 = performance.now();
    press(hello, "Run");
    await until(() => hello.dataset.state === "done", 60000);
    const h = hello.querySelector(".run").textContent;
    say("Run: " + (h.includes("Hello, world!") ? "ok" : "FAILED " + JSON.stringify(h.slice(0, 200))) + " (" + Math.round(performance.now() - t0) + " ms)");
    const ask = box("ch01-first-steps#2");
    press(ask, "Run");
    await until(() => ask.dataset.state === "waiting", 60000);
    const field = ask.querySelector("input.answer");
    if (field) {
      field.value = "Ada";
      field.dispatchEvent(new KeyboardEvent("keydown", { key: "Enter", bubbles: true }));
      await until(() => ask.dataset.state === "done", 60000);
    }
    const a = ask.querySelector(".run").textContent.replace(/\n$/, "");
    say("Input: " + (a === "Your name? Ada\nHello, Ada!" ? "ok" : "FAILED " + JSON.stringify(a.slice(0, 200))));
    const m = box("ch01-first-steps#5"), code = m.querySelector("textarea.code");
    code.value = SOLUTION;
    code.dispatchEvent(new Event("input"));
    press(m, "Check");
    await until(() => m.querySelector(".verdict")?.dataset.done === "yes", 60000);
    const v = m.querySelector(".verdict p.summary")?.textContent ?? "(no verdict)";
    say("Check: " + (v === "Passed all 4 cases." ? "ok" : "FAILED " + JSON.stringify(v)));
  } catch (e) { say("probe stopped: " + e); }
  say("page errors: " + (errors.length ? errors.join(" | ") : "none"));
});
</script>
```

`build/.work/review-spike/stage.mjs`:

```js
// Throwaway (Plan 5, Task 10, Step 1): stages the spike's page and its files under build/.work/review-spike/files/ and
// writes build/.work/review-spike/files.json, what the Artifact tool's publish takes. Run from the repository root.
import fs from "node:fs";
import path from "node:path";
import { REPO, buildSite } from "../../../web/test/harness.mjs";

const spike = path.join(REPO, "build/.work/review-spike"), out = path.join(spike, "files");
fs.rmSync(out, { recursive: true, force: true });
fs.mkdirSync(out, { recursive: true });
const site = path.join(buildSite(path.join(REPO, "web/test/vol-accept"), "review-spike-site"), "site");
for (const d of ["page", "runner", "runtime"]) fs.cpSync(path.join(site, d), path.join(out, d), { recursive: true });
for (const f of ["app.css", "app.js"]) fs.copyFileSync(path.join(site, f), path.join(out, f));
let html = fs.readFileSync(path.join(site, "vol-accept/ch01-first-steps.html"), "utf8");
for (const [from, to] of [['href="../app.css"', 'href="app.css"'], ['src="../app.js"', 'src="app.js"'],
  ["</head>", "<style>.scratch-tab { display: none !important; }</style>\n</head>"],
  ["<main>\n", "<main>\n" + fs.readFileSync(path.join(spike, "probe.html"), "utf8") + "\n"]]) {
  if (html.split(from).length !== 2) throw new Error(`stage: not exactly one ${from} in the page`);
  html = html.replace(from, to);
}
html = html.replace(/<title>[^<]*<\/title>/, "<title>Box runtime spike</title>");
fs.writeFileSync(path.join(out, "index.html"), html);
const TYPES = { ".css": "text/css", ".js": "text/javascript", ".mjs": "text/javascript", ".wasm": "application/wasm",
  ".bin": "application/octet-stream", ".txt": "text/plain", "": "text/plain" };
const walk = (d) => fs.readdirSync(d, { withFileTypes: true }).flatMap((x) => (x.isDirectory() ? walk(path.join(d, x.name)) : [path.join(d, x.name)]));
const files = {};
for (const f of walk(out).sort()) {
  const rel = path.relative(out, f).split(path.sep).join("/");
  if (rel === "index.html") continue;
  if (!TYPES[path.extname(f)]) throw new Error(`stage: no media type for ${rel}`);
  files[rel] = { from: f, contentType: TYPES[path.extname(f)] };
}
fs.writeFileSync(path.join(spike, "files.json"), JSON.stringify({ file_path: path.join(out, "index.html"), files }, null, 1) + "\n");
console.log(`stage: ${Object.keys(files).length} files beside index.html; files.json written`);
```

Run `node build/.work/review-spike/stage.mjs`. Expected: `stage: <N> files beside index.html; files.json written`,
N about 36. The files map it writes has these keys, each `{ from, contentType }`: `app.css` (text/css), `app.js`
(text/javascript); `page/box.js`, `page/check.js`, `page/compose.js`, `page/jshell-session.js`, `page/replay.js`,
`page/scratchpad.js`, `page/sha256.js`, `page/support.js`, `page/transcript.js`, `page/wire.js` (text/javascript);
`runner/browser-runner.js`, `runner/browser-worker.js`, `runner/doors.js`, `runner/java-line.js`, `runner/tjava-core.js`,
`runner/worker-protocol.js` (text/javascript); `runtime/compiler.wasm`, `runtime/compiler.wasm-deobfuscator.wasm`
(application/wasm); `runtime/compile-classlib-teavm.bin`, `runtime/runtime-classlib-teavm.bin`
(application/octet-stream); `runtime/compiler.wasm-runtime.js`, `runtime/compiler.wasm-runtime.mjs` (text/javascript);
and the runtime's notices, `runtime/CHECKSUMS`, `runtime/NOTICE`, `runtime/NOTICE-*.txt`, `runtime/LICENSE-*.txt`,
`runtime/SOURCES.txt` (text/plain). Nothing under `scratchpad/`, nothing of `vol-accept/` but the page itself.

The controller (not the engineer: only a session with the Artifact tool can) loads the `artifact-design` skill, as the
Artifact tool requires before a first publish, then publishes **privately**:
`Artifact({ action: "publish", file_path: <files.json's file_path>, files: <files.json's files>, icon: "code",
description: "Spike: can a Groundwork box run inside an Artifact?" })`. If the session has a browser tool (Claude in
Chrome or the built-in browser), the controller opens the link on the Mac, taps the button and reads the lines itself.
Then it sends the author:

> The spike for the review page is up (private): <link>. On your iPhone, open it the way you would review a chapter,
> tap "Try Run, input and Check", wait for the "page errors" line, and paste every line into a comment on the page
> (or here). About a minute.

The lines come back as a comment: the publish result's subscription line says whether this session watches the
artifact; if not, the controller watches it with ArtifactComments, and reads the author's comment with it.

**Decision rule, written before the spike runs.** Branch (a), "Run works", if **both** the author's phone (the browser
the review link opens in) **and** a desktop browser print all of `Run: ok`, `Input: ok`, `Check: ok` and
`page errors: none`. Branch (b), "Run does not work", otherwise: any `FAILED` line, `probe stopped`, `module worker:`
anything but `ok`, `java` anything but `ready`, a publish the Artifact tool refused (a media type, a size), or no
`page errors` line two minutes after the tap, on either device. It is also (b) when only the desktop works: the review
is on a phone (D119). Record both devices' lines, word for word, for the branch's commit message. Branch (b) departs from
D19 and DESIGN section 6 ("a page with working Run and Check buttons"), so before building it the controller shows the
author the lines and asks, with two options: (b) as recommended, or reviewing on the Mac with `node web/serve.mjs`
instead; the answer is recorded as a new decision in Step 7. The spike's Artifact stays private; it is deleted only if
the author asks.

- [ ] **Step 2: Write the failing test**

**Branch (a):** `web/test/review.mjs`:

```js
/*
 *  Copyright 2026 Groundwork contributors.
 *
 *  Licensed under the Apache License, Version 2.0 (the "License");
 *  you may not use this file except in compliance with the License.
 *  You may obtain a copy of the License at
 *
 *       http://www.apache.org/licenses/LICENSE-2.0
 *
 *  Unless required by applicable law or agreed to in writing, software
 *  distributed under the License is distributed on an "AS IS" BASIS,
 *  WITHOUT WARRANTIES OR CONDITIONS OF ANY KIND, either express or implied.
 *  See the License for the specific language governing permissions and
 *  limitations under the License.
 */
// web/review.mjs on the acceptance fixture: the review copy of its chapter 1 joins both its pages, carries every file the
// page loads and nothing else (no scratchpad, nothing private, nothing over an Artifact's limits), names only paths
// beside it, and, served from its own folder as an Artifact serves a page and its files, runs a box and checks one.
//   node web/test/review.mjs
import fs from "node:fs";
import path from "node:path";
import { spawnSync } from "node:child_process";
import { REPO, check, done, open } from "./harness.mjs";

const p = spawnSync(process.execPath, ["web/review.mjs", "--volume", "web/test/vol-accept", "ch01"], { cwd: REPO, encoding: "utf8" });
check("node web/review.mjs --volume web/test/vol-accept ch01 exits 0 and says what it wrote",
  p.status === 0 && p.stdout.startsWith("review: build/.work/review/vol-accept-ch01/index.html and "),
  { status: p.status, out: (p.stdout + p.stderr).slice(-600) });
if (p.status !== 0) done();
const out = path.join(REPO, "build/.work/review/vol-accept-ch01");
const site = path.join(REPO, "build/.work/review-site-vol-accept/site");
const plan = JSON.parse(fs.readFileSync(path.join(out, "files.json"), "utf8"));
const walk = (d) => fs.readdirSync(d, { withFileTypes: true }).flatMap((x) => (x.isDirectory() ? walk(path.join(d, x.name)) : [path.join(d, x.name)]));
const rel = (root, f) => path.relative(root, f).split(path.sep).join("/");
const want = ["app.css", "app.js", ...["page", "runner", "runtime"].flatMap((d) => walk(path.join(site, d)).map((f) => rel(site, f)))].sort();
const keys = Object.keys(plan.files).sort();
check("files.json maps exactly app.css, app.js and every file under the built site's page/, runner/ and runtime/",
  JSON.stringify(keys) === JSON.stringify(want) && want.includes("runtime/compiler.wasm"),
  { missing: want.filter((k) => !keys.includes(k)), extra: keys.filter((k) => !want.includes(k)) });
check("each is the built site's own file, byte for byte",
  keys.every((k) => fs.readFileSync(plan.files[k].from).equals(fs.readFileSync(path.join(site, k)))), null);
check("nothing of the scratchpad and nothing private is in it",
  !keys.some((k) => /^scratchpad\/|_solutions|_boxes|_checks|answer-keys/.test(k)) && !fs.existsSync(path.join(out, "scratchpad")), keys);
const sizes = keys.map((k) => [k, fs.statSync(plan.files[k].from).size, plan.files[k].contentType]);
check("every file is within an Artifact's limits (15 MB a binary file, 16 MB a text file and the page), with a media type",
  sizes.every(([, s, t]) => t && s <= (t.startsWith("text/") ? 16e6 : 15e6)) && fs.statSync(plan.file_path).size <= 16e6,
  sizes.filter(([, s]) => s > 15e6));
const html = fs.readFileSync(plan.file_path, "utf8");
const published = JSON.parse(fs.readFileSync(path.join(site, "boxes.json"), "utf8")).filter((b) => /^ch01-/.test(b.page)).map((b) => b.id);
const ids = [...html.matchAll(/ data-box="([^"]+)"/g)].map((m) => m[1]);
check(`the page holds both pages' ${published.length} boxes, chapter then practice, in order`,
  JSON.stringify(ids) === JSON.stringify(published) && ids.some((id) => id.startsWith("ch01-first-steps-practice#")), { ids, published });
check("every path in the page is beside it: no ../ and no other site's address in a src or href",
  !html.includes('"../') && !/(src|href)="(https?:)?\/\//.test(html), html.match(/(src|href)="[^"]*"/g));
check("the scratchpad's tab is hidden, and a note says the scratchpad is on the live site",
  html.includes("<style>.scratch-tab { display: none !important; }</style>") && html.includes("The scratchpad is not here"), null);

// Served from its own folder, as an Artifact serves its page and files: a box runs, and a box on the joined practice
// page checks.
const t = await open("chromium", out, "index.html", { viewport: { width: 390, height: 844 }, isMobile: true });
const box = (id) => t.page.locator(`.box[data-box="${id}"]`);
const until = (fn, arg) => t.page.waitForFunction(fn, arg, { timeout: 30000 }).catch(() => {});
await until(() => document.documentElement.dataset.java);
await box("ch01-first-steps#1").getByRole("button", { name: "Run", exact: true }).click({ timeout: 10000 }).catch(() => {});
await until((id) => document.querySelector(`.box[data-box="${id}"]`).dataset.state === "done", "ch01-first-steps#1");
const ran = await box("ch01-first-steps#1").locator(".run").innerText().catch(() => "");
check("Run works in the copy: the first box prints Hello, world!", ran.includes("Hello, world!"), ran);
const step = box("ch01-first-steps-practice#1");
// The step's first worked solution, as its own reveal holds it (shut, so read as text, not as seen).
const worked = await t.page.locator('.box[data-box="ch01-first-steps-practice#1"] > details.solution > pre').textContent({ timeout: 10000 }).catch(() => "");
await step.locator("textarea.code").fill(worked ?? "");
await step.getByRole("button", { name: "Check", exact: true }).click({ timeout: 10000 }).catch(() => {});
await until((id) => document.querySelector(`.box[data-box="${id}"] .verdict`)?.dataset.done === "yes", "ch01-first-steps-practice#1");
const verdict = await step.locator(".verdict p.summary").innerText().catch(() => "");
check("Check works in the copy, on the practice page's box: its worked solution passes", verdict === "Passed all 2 cases.", verdict);
check("the scratchpad's tab is not shown", !(await t.page.locator(".scratch-tab").isVisible()), null);
check("no request left the copy's folder, and no page error", t.offsite.length === 0 && t.errors.length === 0,
  { offsite: t.offsite, errors: t.errors });
await t.close();
done();
```

**Branch (b):** `web/test/review.mjs`:

```js
/*
 *  (the same Apache-2.0 header as above)
 */
// web/review.mjs, the static copy, on the acceptance fixture: once the acceptance of its chapter 1 passes in all three
// engines, the copy joins both pages with no script, puts under every box what WebKit showed and the box's three-way
// proof, carries every screenshot it names, reads at 390 px, and refuses a chapter whose acceptance failed.
//   node web/test/review.mjs   (runs the fixture's acceptance first, in all three engines: about two minutes)
import fs from "node:fs";
import path from "node:path";
import { spawnSync } from "node:child_process";
import { REPO, check, done, open } from "./harness.mjs";

const node = (...a) => spawnSync(process.execPath, a, { cwd: REPO, encoding: "utf8" });
const acc = node("web/test/acceptance.mjs", "--volume", "web/test/vol-accept", "ch01");
check("the acceptance of the fixture's chapter 1 passes in Chromium, WebKit and Firefox", acc.status === 0, acc.stdout.slice(-600));
const p = node("web/review.mjs", "--volume", "web/test/vol-accept", "ch01");
check("node web/review.mjs --volume web/test/vol-accept ch01 exits 0 and says what it wrote",
  p.status === 0 && p.stdout.startsWith("review: build/.work/review/vol-accept-ch01/index.html and "), (p.stdout + p.stderr).slice(-600));
if (p.status !== 0) done();
const out = path.join(REPO, "build/.work/review/vol-accept-ch01");
const plan = JSON.parse(fs.readFileSync(path.join(out, "files.json"), "utf8"));
const html = fs.readFileSync(plan.file_path, "utf8");
const results = path.join(REPO, "build/.work/acceptance-vol-accept/ch01");
const webkit = JSON.parse(fs.readFileSync(path.join(results, "webkit/results.json"), "utf8"));
const boxes = webkit.pages.flatMap((pg) => pg.boxes);
const esc = (s) => String(s).replace(/&/g, "&amp;").replace(/</g, "&lt;").replace(/>/g, "&gt;").replace(/"/g, "&quot;");
check("the copy has no script, no ../ and no other site's address in a src or href",
  !/<script/i.test(html) && !html.includes('"../') && !/(src|href)="(https?:)?\/\//.test(html), null);
const blocks = [...html.matchAll(/<div class="review" data-for="([^"]+)">/g)].map((m) => m[1]);
check(`a review under each of the ${boxes.length} boxes, chapter then practice, in order`,
  boxes.length > 0 && JSON.stringify(blocks) === JSON.stringify(boxes.map((b) => b.id)), { blocks });
const block = (id) => html.slice(html.indexOf(`<div class="review" data-for="${esc(id)}">`)).split("\n</div>")[0];
check("each review shows what Run showed in WebKit, as the acceptance recorded it",
  boxes.filter((b) => b.run).every((b) => block(b.id).includes(`<pre>${esc(b.run.shown)}</pre>`)), null);
check("each checked box's review lists every answer of its three-way proof, with Check's verdict",
  boxes.filter((b) => b.proof.length).every((b) => b.proof.every((a) =>
    block(b.id).includes(`${esc(a.label)}: should ${a.want}; Check said: ${esc(a.summary)}`))), null);
const keys = Object.keys(plan.files).sort();
const wantKeys = ["app.css", ...boxes.map((b) => `shots/${path.basename(b.shot)}`),
  ...["chromium", "webkit", "firefox"].flatMap((e) => webkit.pages.map((pg) => `full/${pg.page}.${e}.png`))].sort();
check("files.json maps app.css, a screenshot of each box and each page's screenshot in each engine, each a file on disk",
  JSON.stringify(keys) === JSON.stringify(wantKeys) && keys.every((k) => fs.existsSync(plan.files[k].from)), { keys, wantKeys });
const t = await open("chromium", out, "index.html", { viewport: { width: 390, height: 844 }, isMobile: true });
const seen = await t.page.evaluate(() => ({ wide: document.documentElement.scrollWidth,
  images: [...document.images].map((i) => i.complete && i.naturalWidth > 0) }));
check("at 390 px it does not scroll sideways, every image loads, nothing leaves its folder and no page error",
  seen.wide <= 390 && seen.images.length === boxes.length && seen.images.every(Boolean) && t.offsite.length === 0 && t.errors.length === 0,
  { ...seen, offsite: t.offsite, errors: t.errors });
await t.close();
// A chapter whose acceptance failed in one engine is refused, naming the engine.
const firefox = path.join(results, "firefox/results.json"), saved = fs.readFileSync(firefox, "utf8");
fs.writeFileSync(firefox, JSON.stringify({ ...JSON.parse(saved), failed: 1 }));
const refused = node("web/review.mjs", "--volume", "web/test/vol-accept", "ch01");
fs.writeFileSync(firefox, saved);
check("it refuses a chapter whose acceptance failed in an engine, naming the engine",
  refused.status === 1 && refused.stdout.includes("the acceptance of ch01 in firefox has 1 failed check(s)"), refused.stdout);
done();
```

**Branch (a):** `web/review.mjs`:

```js
/*
 *  Copyright 2026 Groundwork contributors.
 *
 *  Licensed under the Apache License, Version 2.0 (the "License");
 *  you may not use this file except in compliance with the License.
 *  You may obtain a copy of the License at
 *
 *       http://www.apache.org/licenses/LICENSE-2.0
 *
 *  Unless required by applicable law or agreed to in writing, software
 *  distributed under the License is distributed on an "AS IS" BASIS,
 *  WITHOUT WARRANTIES OR CONDITIONS OF ANY KIND, either express or implied.
 *  See the License for the specific language governing permissions and
 *  limitations under the License.
 */
// The author's review copy of one chapter (DESIGN section 6, D119), for a private claude.ai Artifact: the chapter's page
// and its practice page as built, joined into one page whose boxes run, check and reveal as on the site, with each file
// that page loads beside it at the same relative path: the page's scripts, the runner, and the boxes' runtime with its
// notices. The scratchpad stays behind: its jdk.zip (22 MB) is more than an Artifact takes in one file (15 MB), so its
// tab is hidden and a note says where it is. It refuses to write a file over an Artifact's limits.
// Writes build/.work/review/<chNN>/ (build/.work/review/<slug>-<chNN>/ for another volume): index.html, the files beside
// it, and files.json, which holds what the Artifact tool's publish takes: file_path and the files map, with absolute
// paths and each file's media type.
//   node web/review.mjs [--volume <dir>] <chNN>
import fs from "node:fs";
import path from "node:path";
import { REPO, buildSite } from "./test/harness.mjs";

const USAGE = "usage: node web/review.mjs [--volume <dir>] <chNN>";
const args = process.argv.slice(2);
let volumeDir = path.join(REPO, "volumes/vol1");
if (args[0] === "--volume") {
  if (!args[1]) { console.log(USAGE); process.exit(2); }
  volumeDir = path.resolve(REPO, args[1]);
  args.splice(0, 2);
}
const [ch] = args;
if (args.length !== 1 || !/^ch\d+$/.test(ch)) { console.log(USAGE); process.exit(2); }
const slug = path.basename(volumeDir);
const pages = ["content", "practice"].flatMap((d) => {
  const dir = path.join(volumeDir, d);
  return fs.existsSync(dir) ? fs.readdirSync(dir).filter((f) => f.startsWith(`${ch}-`) && f.endsWith(".md")).sort().map((f) => f.slice(0, -3)) : [];
});
if (!pages.length) { console.log(`no chapter ${ch} in ${path.relative(REPO, volumeDir)}`); process.exit(2); }

const site = path.join(buildSite(volumeDir, `review-site-${slug}`), "site");
const out = path.join(REPO, "build/.work/review", slug === "vol1" ? ch : `${slug}-${ch}`);
fs.rmSync(out, { recursive: true, force: true });
fs.mkdirSync(out, { recursive: true });
// What the page loads, copied whole from the built site, so this never has to follow the build's own lists: the page's
// scripts, the runner, and the boxes' runtime with its notices and CHECKSUMS.
for (const d of ["page", "runner", "runtime"]) fs.cpSync(path.join(site, d), path.join(out, d), { recursive: true });
for (const f of ["app.css", "app.js"]) fs.copyFileSync(path.join(site, f), path.join(out, f));

// Each edit must find exactly one place in the built page; anything else means the page's template changed under this
// script and the copy would be wrong, so it stops.
function once(html, from, to) {
  const at = html.indexOf(from);
  if (at < 0 || html.indexOf(from, at + 1) >= 0) throw new Error(`review: not exactly one ${JSON.stringify(from)} in the built page`);
  return html.slice(0, at) + to + html.slice(at + from.length);
}
function cut(html, start, end) {
  const at = html.indexOf(start), to = html.indexOf(end, at);
  if (at < 0 || to < 0 || html.indexOf(start, at + 1) >= 0) throw new Error(`review: not exactly one ${JSON.stringify(start)} in the built page`);
  return html.slice(0, at) + html.slice(to + end.length);
}
const built = (p) => fs.readFileSync(path.join(site, slug, `${p}.html`), "utf8");
const mainOf = (html) => {
  const a = html.indexOf("<main>\n"), b = html.lastIndexOf("\n</main>");
  if (a < 0 || b < a) throw new Error("review: no <main> in the built page");
  return html.slice(a + "<main>\n".length, b);
};
const n = Number(ch.slice(2));
const NOTE = `<p class="note">A review copy of chapter ${n}${pages.length > 1 ? " and its practice page" : ""}, as built. ` +
  "Run, Check and Show a solution work here as on the site. The scratchpad is not here: what it downloads (22 MB) is " +
  "more than this copy can hold in one file. It is on the live site, https://mmmugh.github.io/groundwork/, once the " +
  "chapter is published.</p>\n";
let html = built(pages[0]);
html = once(html, '<link rel="stylesheet" href="../app.css">',
  '<link rel="stylesheet" href="app.css">\n<style>.scratch-tab { display: none !important; }</style>');
html = once(html, '<script type="module" src="../app.js"></script>', '<script type="module" src="app.js"></script>');
html = once(html, html.match(/<title>[^<]*<\/title>/)?.[0] ?? "<title>", `<title>Chapter ${n} review</title>`);
html = cut(html, '<header class="top">', "</header>\n"); // its home link leads off the copy
html = cut(html, '<nav class="pager">', "</nav>\n"); // so do Previous and Next; the practice page follows below
html = once(html, "<main>\n", `<main>\n${NOTE}`);
html = once(html, "\n</main>", `\n${pages.slice(1).map((p) => mainOf(built(p))).join("\n")}\n</main>`);
// Task 4's quiz and code links point at files this copy does not carry: the copy has none.
html = html.replace(/<ul class="chapter-links">[\s\S]*?<\/ul>\n?/g, "");
fs.writeFileSync(path.join(out, "index.html"), html);

// The Artifact tool's limits (its own description): 15 MB a binary file, 16 MB the page and each text file.
const TYPES = { ".css": "text/css", ".js": "text/javascript", ".mjs": "text/javascript", ".wasm": "application/wasm",
  ".bin": "application/octet-stream", ".txt": "text/plain", "": "text/plain" };
const walk = (d) => fs.readdirSync(d, { withFileTypes: true }).flatMap((x) => (x.isDirectory() ? walk(path.join(d, x.name)) : [path.join(d, x.name)]));
const files = {};
for (const f of walk(out).sort()) {
  const rel = path.relative(out, f).split(path.sep).join("/");
  const type = TYPES[path.extname(f)], size = fs.statSync(f).size;
  if (rel !== "index.html" && !type) throw new Error(`review: no media type for ${rel}`);
  const limit = rel === "index.html" || type.startsWith("text/") ? 16e6 : 15e6;
  if (size > limit) throw new Error(`review: ${rel} is ${size} bytes, more than an Artifact takes in one file (${limit / 1e6} MB)`);
  if (rel !== "index.html") files[rel] = { from: f, contentType: type };
}
fs.writeFileSync(path.join(out, "files.json"), JSON.stringify({ file_path: path.join(out, "index.html"), files }, null, 1) + "\n");
console.log(`review: ${path.relative(REPO, out)}/index.html and ${Object.keys(files).length} files beside it (files.json)`);
```

```
and `files.json`, the file map the claude.ai Artifact tool publishes it from. The scratchpad is not in it.
```

**Branch (b):** `web/review.mjs`:

```js
/*
 *  (the same Apache-2.0 header as branch (a)'s, written in full)
 */
// The author's review copy of one chapter (DESIGN section 6, D119), for a private claude.ai Artifact, where a box cannot
// run (Plan 5's spike): the chapter's page and its practice page as built, joined into one page with no script, and
// under each box what the acceptance (web/test/acceptance.mjs) saw it do in WebKit, Safari's engine as on an iPhone, at
// 390 px: the box after its Run, what Run showed, each answer Check was given with its verdict, and whether Show a
// solution showed the worked solution. At the top, each engine's result and its full-page screenshots. It refuses a
// chapter whose acceptance has not passed in all three engines.
// Writes build/.work/review/<chNN>/ (build/.work/review/<slug>-<chNN>/ for another volume): index.html, app.css, shots/,
// full/, and files.json, which holds what the Artifact tool's publish takes: file_path and the files map, with absolute
// paths and each file's media type.
//   node web/review.mjs [--volume <dir>] <chNN>
import fs from "node:fs";
import path from "node:path";
import { REPO, buildSite } from "./test/harness.mjs";

const ENGINES = { chromium: "Chromium", webkit: "WebKit", firefox: "Firefox" };
const USAGE = "usage: node web/review.mjs [--volume <dir>] <chNN>";
const args = process.argv.slice(2);
let volumeDir = path.join(REPO, "volumes/vol1");
if (args[0] === "--volume") {
  if (!args[1]) { console.log(USAGE); process.exit(2); }
  volumeDir = path.resolve(REPO, args[1]);
  args.splice(0, 2);
}
const [ch] = args;
if (args.length !== 1 || !/^ch\d+$/.test(ch)) { console.log(USAGE); process.exit(2); }
const slug = path.basename(volumeDir);
const pages = ["content", "practice"].flatMap((d) => {
  const dir = path.join(volumeDir, d);
  return fs.existsSync(dir) ? fs.readdirSync(dir).filter((f) => f.startsWith(`${ch}-`) && f.endsWith(".md")).sort().map((f) => f.slice(0, -3)) : [];
});
if (!pages.length) { console.log(`no chapter ${ch} in ${path.relative(REPO, volumeDir)}`); process.exit(2); }

// The acceptance, in all three engines, and passed: a chapter is reviewed only after it.
const results = path.join(REPO, "build/.work", slug === "vol1" ? "acceptance" : `acceptance-${slug}`, ch);
const report = {};
for (const e of Object.keys(ENGINES)) {
  const f = path.join(results, e, "results.json");
  if (!fs.existsSync(f)) {
    console.log(`review: no acceptance of ${ch} in ${e}: run node web/test/acceptance.mjs` +
      `${slug === "vol1" ? "" : ` --volume ${path.relative(REPO, volumeDir)}`} ${ch} ${e} first`);
    process.exit(1);
  }
  report[e] = JSON.parse(fs.readFileSync(f, "utf8"));
  if (report[e].failed !== 0 || report[e].checks === 0) {
    console.log(`review: the acceptance of ${ch} in ${e} has ${report[e].failed} failed check(s) of ${report[e].checks}: ` +
      "a chapter is reviewed only once it passes");
    process.exit(1);
  }
}

const site = path.join(buildSite(volumeDir, `review-site-${slug}`), "site");
const out = path.join(REPO, "build/.work/review", slug === "vol1" ? ch : `${slug}-${ch}`);
fs.rmSync(out, { recursive: true, force: true });
for (const d of ["shots", "full"]) fs.mkdirSync(path.join(out, d), { recursive: true });
fs.copyFileSync(path.join(site, "app.css"), path.join(out, "app.css"));
const esc = (s) => String(s).replace(/&/g, "&amp;").replace(/</g, "&lt;").replace(/>/g, "&gt;").replace(/"/g, "&quot;");
const built = (p) => fs.readFileSync(path.join(site, slug, `${p}.html`), "utf8");
const mainOf = (html) => {
  const a = html.indexOf("<main>\n"), b = html.lastIndexOf("\n</main>");
  if (a < 0 || b < a) throw new Error("review: no <main> in the built page");
  return html.slice(a + "<main>\n".length, b);
};

// What the acceptance saw one box do in WebKit, written under the box.
const seen = new Map(report.webkit.pages.flatMap((p) => p.boxes.map((b) => [b.id, b])));
const shots = path.join(results, "webkit");
function review(id) {
  const b = seen.get(id);
  if (!b) throw new Error(`review: the acceptance in webkit saw no box ${id}`);
  const parts = [];
  if (b.shot) {
    const name = path.basename(b.shot);
    fs.copyFileSync(path.join(shots, b.shot), path.join(out, "shots", name));
    parts.push(`<img src="shots/${esc(name)}" alt="${esc(id)} in WebKit at 390 px, after its Run" width="390">`);
  }
  if (b.skipped) parts.push(`<p>Not run: ${esc(b.skipped)}</p>`);
  if (b.run) parts.push(`<p>Run showed (${(b.run.ms / 1000).toFixed(1)} s):</p>\n<pre>${esc(b.run.shown)}</pre>`);
  if (b.proof.length) parts.push("<p>Check, three ways:</p>\n<ul>" + b.proof.map((a) => `<li>${esc(a.label)}: should ${a.want}; ` +
    `Check said: ${esc(a.summary)} (${(a.ms / 1000).toFixed(1)} s)<details><summary>What was typed</summary>` +
    `<pre>${esc(a.answer)}</pre></details></li>`).join("\n") + "</ul>");
  if (b.noCheck != null) parts.push(`<p>No Check; the box says: ${esc(b.noCheck)}</p>`);
  if (b.reveal) parts.push("<p>Show a solution opened this box's first worked solution; every other box's stayed shut.</p>");
  return `\n<div class="review" data-for="${esc(id)}">\n${parts.join("\n")}\n</div>`;
}
// Each box of a page's body, its whole element (a method check's case list is a div inside it), then its review.
function underEachBox(body) {
  let html = "", at = 0;
  const start = /<div class="box" id="([^"]+)"/g;
  for (let m; (m = start.exec(body));) {
    const tag = /<div\b|<\/div>/g;
    tag.lastIndex = m.index;
    let depth = 0, end = -1;
    for (let t; (t = tag.exec(body));) {
      depth += t[0] === "</div>" ? -1 : 1;
      if (depth === 0) { end = tag.lastIndex; break; }
    }
    if (end < 0) throw new Error(`review: box ${m[1]} has no closing </div>`);
    html += body.slice(at, end) + review(m[1]);
    at = start.lastIndex = end;
  }
  return html + body.slice(at);
}

const n = Number(ch.slice(2));
const summary = Object.entries(ENGINES).map(([e, name]) => {
  const links = report[e].pages.map((p) => {
    const file = `${p.page}.${e}.png`;
    fs.copyFileSync(path.join(results, e, p.shot), path.join(out, "full", file));
    return `<a href="full/${esc(file)}">${esc(p.page)}</a>`;
  });
  return `<li>${name}: ${report[e].checks} checks, all passed, in ${report[e].seconds} s on ${esc(report[e].platform)}; ` +
    `the whole pages: ${links.join(", ")}</li>`;
}).join("\n");
const NOTE = `<p class="note">A static review copy of chapter ${n}${pages.length > 1 ? " and its practice page" : ""}, as ` +
  "built. Nothing runs here: an Artifact cannot run a box (Plan 5's spike). Under each box is what the acceptance saw " +
  "in WebKit, Safari's engine as on an iPhone, at 390 px: the box after its Run, what Run showed, each answer Check " +
  "was given and its verdict, and whether Show a solution showed the worked solution.</p>\n";
const STYLE = ".review { border-left: 3px solid var(--rule); margin: 0.5rem 0 1.5rem; padding-left: 0.75rem; }\n" +
  ".review img { display: block; max-width: 100%; height: auto; }\n.review pre { overflow-x: auto; }";
const body = pages.map((p) => underEachBox(mainOf(built(p)))).join("\n");
const html = "<!doctype html>\n<html lang=\"en\">\n<head>\n<meta charset=\"utf-8\">\n" +
  "<meta name=\"viewport\" content=\"width=device-width, initial-scale=1\">\n" +
  `<title>Chapter ${n} review</title>\n<link rel="stylesheet" href="app.css">\n<style>\n${STYLE}\n</style>\n</head>\n` +
  `<body>\n<main>\n${NOTE}<ul class="review-summary">\n${summary}\n</ul>\n${body}\n</main>\n</body>\n</html>\n`;
fs.writeFileSync(path.join(out, "index.html"), html);

// The Artifact tool's limits (its own description): 15 MB a binary file, 16 MB the page and each text file.
const TYPES = { ".css": "text/css", ".png": "image/png" };
const walk = (d) => fs.readdirSync(d, { withFileTypes: true }).flatMap((x) => (x.isDirectory() ? walk(path.join(d, x.name)) : [path.join(d, x.name)]));
const files = {};
for (const f of walk(out).sort()) {
  const rel = path.relative(out, f).split(path.sep).join("/");
  const type = TYPES[path.extname(f)], size = fs.statSync(f).size;
  if (rel !== "index.html" && !type) throw new Error(`review: no media type for ${rel}`);
  const limit = rel === "index.html" || type === "text/css" ? 16e6 : 15e6;
  if (size > limit) throw new Error(`review: ${rel} is ${size} bytes, more than an Artifact takes in one file (${limit / 1e6} MB)`);
  if (rel !== "index.html") files[rel] = { from: f, contentType: type };
}
fs.writeFileSync(path.join(out, "files.json"), JSON.stringify({ file_path: path.join(out, "index.html"), files }, null, 1) + "\n");
console.log(`review: ${path.relative(REPO, out)}/index.html and ${Object.keys(files).length} files beside it (files.json)`);
```

`ci/everything.sh`, after line 35 (`step web-replay-page-test …`), add (the Mac only: the test needs all three engines,
and each weekly CI job has one):

```bash
step web-review node web/test/review.mjs
```

- [ ] **Step 5: Run; expected PASS**

`node web/test/review.mjs`. Expected, branch (a): every check `ok`, `12 check(s), 0 failed`, exit 0. Branch (b): every
check `ok`, `9 check(s), 0 failed`, exit 0 (about two minutes, most of it the fixture's acceptance).

Then the real thing, once the course has a chapter (until then, the fixture): `node web/review.mjs --volume web/test/vol-accept ch01`,
`cat build/.work/review/vol-accept-ch01/files.json | head -5` shows `file_path` and the first entries, and opening

at `http://127.0.0.1:8741/index.html` at a phone's width reads as the chapter. The wider suites: `$J build/BuildTest.java`
(ends `<N> test(s), 0 failed`), `node ci/test.mjs` (ends `the ci scripts keep their promises`), and
`node web/test/acceptance.mjs --volume web/test/vol-accept all chromium` (`66 check(s), 0 failed`).

- [ ] **Step 6: Prove the gate by breaking it on purpose**

Save `cp web/review.mjs build/.work/review-breaks.mjs` (after `mkdir -p build/.work`). Each break alone, then
`node web/test/review.mjs`; each must end with the named check `FAIL` and exit 1. Restore with
`cp build/.work/review-breaks.mjs web/review.mjs` and `cmp build/.work/review-breaks.mjs web/review.mjs` (silent).

Branch (a):

| # | Break (exact edit in web/review.mjs) | Expected |
|---|---|---|
| 1 | The runtime left behind: `["page", "runner", "runtime"]` becomes `["page", "runner"]` | `FAIL  files.json maps exactly …` and `FAIL  Run works in the copy …` and `FAIL  Check works in the copy …`; exit 1 |
| 2 | A path that leaves the folder: in the first `once`, `href="app.css"` becomes `href="../app.css"` (the page still looks right: a URL's `../` stops at the root, which is why the check reads the text) | `FAIL  every path in the page is beside it …`; `12 check(s), 1 failed`; exit 1 |
| 3 | The scratchpad's tab shown: delete `\n<style>.scratch-tab { display: none !important; }</style>` from the first `once`'s replacement | `FAIL  the scratchpad's tab is hidden, and a note …` and `FAIL  the scratchpad's tab is not shown`; `12 check(s), 2 failed`; exit 1 |
| 4 | The scratchpad copied: `["page", "runner", "runtime"]` becomes `["page", "runner", "runtime", "scratchpad"]` | review.mjs stops with `review: …scratchpad/…` (no media type, or over 15 MB); `FAIL  node web/review.mjs … exits 0 …`; `1 check(s), 1 failed`; exit 1 |
| 5 | The practice page dropped: `pages.slice(1).map((p) => mainOf(built(p))).join("\n")` becomes `""` | `FAIL  the page holds both pages' … boxes …` and `FAIL  Check works in the copy, on the practice page's box …`; `12 check(s), 2 failed`; exit 1 |

Branch (b):

| # | Break (exact edit in web/review.mjs) | Expected |
|---|---|---|
| 1 | A script left in: `</main>\n</body>` in the page's text becomes `</main>\n<script type="module" src="app.js"></script>\n</body>` | `FAIL  the copy has no script …`; `9 check(s), 1 failed`; exit 1 |
| 2 | Chromium's runs shown, not WebKit's: `report.webkit.pages` (in `seen`) becomes `report.chromium.pages` | `FAIL  each review shows what Run showed in WebKit …` (box #3: Chromium shows the at line, WebKit does not, D42); `9 check(s), 1 failed`; exit 1 |
| 3 | No review under a predict box: first line of `review` becomes `if (seen.get(id)?.kind === "predict") return "";` | `FAIL  a review under each of the 12 boxes …`; exit 1 |
| 4 | A failed acceptance let through: delete `report[e].failed !== 0 || ` | `FAIL  it refuses a chapter whose acceptance failed in an engine …`; `9 check(s), 1 failed`; exit 1 |
| 5 | The screenshots left behind: delete the `fs.copyFileSync(path.join(shots, b.shot), …)` line | `FAIL  files.json maps app.css, a screenshot of each box …` and `FAIL  at 390 px … every image loads …`; `9 check(s), 2 failed`; exit 1 |

After the last restore: `cmp build/.work/review-breaks.mjs web/review.mjs && echo identical` prints `identical`, and
`node web/test/review.mjs` passes again.

branch (b):

Branch (a)'s message (branch (b)'s follows the same shape: what the copy is, the spike's lines, its five breaks):

```
feat(web): the author's review copy of a chapter, for a private Artifact, runs its boxes (Plan 5)

The author reviews chapter 1, and any chapter whose acceptance is less certain, as a private claude.ai Artifact with
comments (D119, DESIGN section 6). Plan 5's spike published a built page and the boxes' runtime as an Artifact's files,
every path relative, with a probe that pressed the page's own buttons; on the author's phone and on the Mac it printed
<the probe's lines, both devices>. So the review copy runs: web/review.mjs joins a chapter's page and its practice page
into one page, copies the page's scripts, the runner and the runtime with its notices beside it, hides the scratchpad's
tab (its 22 MB download is over an Artifact's 15 MB a file) with a note saying where it is, and writes files.json, the
map the Artifact tool publishes from. It refuses a file over an Artifact's limits.

Proven by breaking it (node web/test/review.mjs, exit 1 each):
- the runtime left behind: the file map, Run and Check fail
- a path leaving the folder: the relative-paths check fails (the page still looks right)
- the scratchpad's tab shown: both tab checks fail
- the scratchpad copied: review.mjs refuses it
- the practice page dropped: the box list and the practice box's Check fail

Co-Authored-By: Claude Opus 5.5 <noreply@anthropic.com>
```

- [ ] **Step 8: Use it, chapter by chapter (the controller)**

After a chapter has passed every gate, its fresh-reviewer panel, and `node web/test/acceptance.mjs chNN` in all three
engines with `0 failed`, the controller applies these triggers. **The author is shown the chapter only when one
fires.** `BASE` is the commit the controller noted when the chapter's work began.

| # | Trigger: acceptance might be less certain | How the controller tells |
|---|---|---|
| T1 | Chapter 1: the pilot, always shown. | `chNN` is `ch01`. |
| T2 | A section redesigned beyond the recorded decisions: a section added, dropped, merged or moved, or a project or practice story replaced, that no DESIGN.md section 2 or 3 bullet and no D-number makes. Wording changes (`print` to `IO.println`) do not count. | Both outlines side by side: `grep -n '^##' ~/python_foundations/volumes/vol1-foundations/content/chNN-*.md ~/python_foundations/volumes/vol1-foundations/practice/chNN-*.md` and the same over `volumes/vol1/content/chNN-*.md volumes/vol1/practice/chNN-*.md`; every difference must cite its decision in the writer's report, or the trigger fires. |
| T3 | An exercise left with `noCheck`. | The command below prints `noCheck:` with box ids. |
| T4 | A reviewer finding resolved by judgment: closed without a change a gate or test verifies (dismissed, accepted as a known cost, or settled by a ruling). | The panel's ledger for the chapter. |
| T5 | A gate exception added: a `varies` box (its output is compared with nothing), an entry added to any gate's own list of exemptions or known differences, or any file outside `volumes/vol1/` changed while the chapter was written (a gate, a test, a page script, the runtime, the build). | The command below prints `varies:`; and `git diff --name-only "$BASE" -- . ':(exclude)volumes/vol1'; git ls-files --others --exclude-standard -- . ':(exclude)volumes/vol1'` prints any path. |
| T6 | An acceptance check that had to be loosened: `web/test/acceptance.mjs` changed since `BASE`, a box the acceptance skipped, or an engine that passed only on a rerun with nothing changed. | The path list above includes `web/test/acceptance.mjs`; the command below prints `skipped:` with a box; the controller's own run log. |
| T7 | A box near the page's deadline: any Run or Check that took more than 2.5 s (half the page's 5 s) in any engine on the Mac, which a slower phone may stop (the page and the build's gates allow different times). | The command below prints `slow:` with entries. |

```
node -e '
const fs = require("fs"), ch = process.argv[1];
const decl = JSON.parse(fs.readFileSync("volumes/vol1/content/_boxes.json", "utf8"));
const mine = Object.entries(decl).filter(([id]) => id.startsWith(ch + "-"));
const runs = ["chromium", "webkit", "firefox"].map((e) => JSON.parse(fs.readFileSync(`build/.work/acceptance/${ch}/${e}/results.json`, "utf8")));
const boxes = runs.flatMap((r) => r.pages.flatMap((p) => p.boxes.map((b) => ({ ...b, engine: r.engine }))));
const slow = boxes.flatMap((b) => [b.run, ...b.proof].filter((x) => x && x.ms > 2500).map((x) => `${b.engine} ${b.id} ${x.label ?? "Run"} ${x.ms} ms`));
console.log("noCheck: " + (mine.filter(([, d]) => d.noCheck).map(([id]) => id).join(", ") || "none"));
console.log("varies: " + (mine.filter(([, d]) => d.varies).map(([id]) => id).join(", ") || "none"));
console.log("skipped: " + ([...new Set(boxes.filter((b) => b.skipped).map((b) => `${b.engine} ${b.id}`))].join(", ") || "none"));
console.log("slow: " + (slow.join(", ") || "none"));
' ch03
```

**No trigger fired** (and not chapter 1): the controller tells the author in one line, and commits the chapter as its
task says (locally; a push, which deploys Pages, waits for the author's go as always):

> Chapter N, <title>: every gate, the review panel and the acceptance passed (<C> checks in Chromium, WebKit and
> Firefox at 390 px); no trigger fired, so it is committed without a page review. Its screenshots are in
> build/.work/acceptance/chNN/.

**A trigger fired, or chapter 1:** `node web/review.mjs chNN`; then, with the `artifact-design` skill loaded (the
Artifact tool's rule before a first publish; the copy keeps the course's own styles, which already set light and dark
colors), `Artifact({ action: "publish", file_path, files, icon: "code", description: "Chapter N of Groundwork, built,
for the author's private review." })` with `file_path` and `files` from `build/.work/review/chNN/files.json`. The
Artifact stays **private** and is never shared: in branch (b) it shows the chapter's mistakes and worked solutions.
The controller sends the author:

> Chapter N, <title>, is ready for your review (private): <link>
> Shown because: <each trigger that fired, in a few words, with its box ids or files>.
> Acceptance on the Mac: passed in Chromium, WebKit and Firefox at 390 px; <C> checks, <B> boxes run, <P> answers
> proved three ways, <R> solutions revealed; <c> s, <w> s, <f> s.
> In the page: [branch (a)] Run, Check and Show a solution work as on the site; the scratchpad is not in this copy.
> [branch (b)] nothing runs; under each box is what WebKit showed after Run and each Check's verdict.
> Comment on anything in the page. When it can be committed, say "approve chapter N" here.

**Comments come back** through the Artifact's comment threads. The publish result's subscription line says whether
this session watches the artifact; if it does not, the controller watches it with ArtifactComments (load it first:
ToolSearch `select:ArtifactComments`). Each comment the author sends arrives as a notice; the controller reads the
thread with ArtifactComments and answers in that thread: what it changed and where, or why it changed nothing. It makes
the change, reruns the gates and `node web/test/acceptance.mjs chNN`, rebuilds the copy with `node web/review.mjs chNN`,
and publishes again from the same `file_path` (the same link; from a later session, pass the artifact's `url` after
reading it with `action: "read"`). A comment is the author's request, never the approval: the chapter is committed only
after the author's own message in the conversation approves it (D119).

---

## Part 2: the chapters

Each chapter task runs "The chapter procedure" above, as one workflow (D120): a writer (Opus) for steps 2-6, the gates
and acceptance run by a gate agent (Sonnet) that reports exact counts, four review lenses (Sonnet) and a triage (Opus),
then the fix loop. The controller assembles the input pack, applies the certainty triggers, commits, and asks the author
before each push. Python sources are named relative to ~/python_foundations/volumes/vol1-foundations/ (P = that
folder). Every chapter task's acceptance criteria, besides its own: the gates of step 7 at 0 problems, acceptance at 0
failed in all three engines, no open Critical or Important finding, the commit made.
